# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-09-23 through 2026-09-29  
**Updated:** 2026-09-23T10:48:59+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-01-21

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-26 — Brooklyn Book Festival Children's Day + Theodora

**Brooklyn Book Festival Children's Day, then a meal.** Mediterranean seafood at Theodora. It is a 19-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=209+Joralemon+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=7+Greene+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.theodorabk.com/)

### 2026-09-27 — Brooklyn Book Festival Day and Literary Marketplace + Gage & Tollner

**Brooklyn Book Festival Day and Literary Marketplace, then a meal.** American steakhouse and seafood at Gage & Tollner. It is a 4-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=209+Joralemon+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=372+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.gageandtollner.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.gageandtollner.com/reservations)

### 2026-09-27 — Smorgasburg Prospect Park + Little Grenjai

**Smorgasburg Prospect Park, then a meal.** Thai-American at Little Grenjai. It is 2.1 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park+%28enter+at+Lincoln+Road%29%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=477+Gates+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11216&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.smorgasburg.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.littlegrenjai.com/)

### 2026-09-28 — Theodora + A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan-American

**Dinner, then A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan-American.** Mediterranean seafood at Theodora. It is 2.1 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan-American starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=7+Greene+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=159+Pioneer+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43230698-a-winged-victory-for-the-sullen-at-pioneer-works) · [Restaurant](https://www.theodorabk.com/)

### 2026-09-29 — Gage & Tollner + A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan-American

**Dinner, then A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan-American.** American steakhouse and seafood at Gage & Tollner. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan-American starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=372+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=159+Pioneer+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43230772-a-winged-victory-for-the-sullen-at-pioneer-works) · [Restaurant](https://www.gageandtollner.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.gageandtollner.com/reservations)

### 2026-09-23 — Settlers of Catan Social + Elder

**Settlers of Catan Social, then dinner.** British Indian chophouse at Elder. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Between+W+40th%E2%80%93W+42nd+St+and+5th%E2%80%936th+Ave&destination=20+Hudson+Yards%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bryantpark.org/calendar/event/settlers-of-catan-social-II/2026-09-23) · [Restaurant](https://www.eldernyc.com/)

9:00 AM · outdoors · Free · Prospect Park · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 67.0

**Timing:** Wednesday 9am-noon
**Practical note:** Outdoor stewardship with bending and lifting; NWS forecast is breezy but dry, high near 66.
**Why now:** Open house is scheduled on September 23 in the current window.
**On-sale watch:** Prospect Park asks participants to register for EcoZone opportunities.

4:00 PM · markets · Free · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 55.4  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.

No candidate should carry an independently verified Michelin award claim. Eater states that Mexico City’s El Califa de León was the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024, but the Michelin Guide page was blocked by an AWS WAF challenge in direct checking, so treat that only as an Eater-reported background fact, not a verified award field.

### Union Square Cafe lunch and midday bar-bites menu

More restaurants — Union Square Cafe

Official lunch menu includes snack-sized items and pasta half portions, useful for a less expensive visit to a classic USHG dining room.

Price: Duck liver profiterole $6; garlic potato chips $9; rigatoni alla Norma 24 | 34; spaghetti alle vongole 27 | 37; lunch entrees around $29–$35  
Schedule: Lunch menu listed on official page; the page also states midday service is daily 2:30-5 PM and notes lunch is offered Monday-Friday.  
Conditions: Ordinary restaurant menu, not a discount or special prix-fixe. Official menu shows duck liver profiterole $6, garlic potato chips $9, rigatoni alla Norma 24 | 34, spaghetti alle vongole 27 | 37, and lunch entrees including turkey sandwich $29, burger $32, chicken paillard $34, trout and pork Milanese $35.
